- 参数: `{"content": "# Go (Golang) 学习手册\n\n> 编写人: Agnes | 更新时间: 2026-10-20 | 基于 Go 官方文档 (go.dev) 及社区资源整理\n\n## 一、基础语法\n\n### 1.1 Hello World 与基本结构\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    fmt.Println(\"Hello, Go!\")\n}\n```\n\n### 1.2 变量与类型\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    // 显式类型声明\n    var name string = \"Agnes\"\n    var age int = 25\n    var pi float64 = 3.14159\n    var isDev bool = true\n\n    // 短变量声明 (函数内)\n    msg := \"Hello\"\n    count := 42\n    ratio := 3.14\n\n    // 类型推断\n    x := 10       // int\n    y := \"text\"   // string\n    z := true     // bool\n\n    fmt.Printf(\"name=%s, age=%d, pi=%.3f\\n\", name, age, pi)\n    fmt.Println(msg, count, ratio, x, y, z)\n}\n```\n\n### 1.3 零值\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    // Go 所有变量都有零值\n    var a int       // 0\n    var b string    // \"\"\n    var c bool      // false\n    var d float64   // 0\n    var e []int     // nil\n    var f map[string]int // nil\n\n    fmt.Println(a, b, c, d, e, f)\n}\n```\n\n## 二、控制流\n\n### 2.1 条件与循环\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    // if-else (无括号，花括号必须)\n    score := 85\n    if score >= 90 {\n        fmt.Println(\"Grade: A\")\n    } else if score >= 80 {\n        fmt.Println(\"Grade: B\")\n    } else {\n        fmt.Println(\"Grade: C\")\n    }\n\n    // for - Go 唯一的循环结构\n    // 经典 for\n    for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {\n        fmt.Print(i, \" \")\n    }\n\n    // while 风格\n    n := 1\n    for n < 100 {\n        n *= 2\n    }\n\n    // range\n    fruits := []string{\"apple\", \"banana\", \"cherry\"}\n    for index, val := range fruits {\n        fmt.Printf(\"[%d] %s\\n\", index, val)\n    }\n\n    // 映射遍历\n    ages := map[string]int{\"Alice\": 30, \"Bob\": 25}\n    for name, age := range ages {\n        fmt.Printf(\"%s: %d\\n\", name, age)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2.2 switch 与 goto\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\nfunc main() {\n    day := \"Monday\"\n    switch day {\n    case \"Monday\", \"Tuesday\", \"Wednesday\", \"Thursday\", \"Friday\":\n        fmt.Println(\"Weekday\")\n    case \"Saturday\", \"Sunday\":\n        fmt.Println(\"Weekend\")\n    default:\n        fmt.Println(\"Unknown day\")\n    }\n\n    // 无表达式 switch\n    switch {\n    case 2 < 1:\n        fmt.Println(\"false\")\n    case 2 > 1:\n        fmt.Println(\"true\")\n    }\n\n    // 类型 switch\n    var val interface{} = \"hello\"\n    switch v := val.(type) {\n    case int:\n        fmt.Println(\"int:\", v)\n    case string:\n        fmt.Println(\"string:\", v)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## 三、函数\n\n### 3.1 多返回值与 defer\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"fmt\"\n    \"os\"\n)\n\n// 多返回值\nfunc divide(a, b float64) (float64, error) {\n    if b == 0 {\n        return 0, fmt.Errorf(\"division by zero\")\n    }\n    return a / b, nil\n}\n\n// defer - 延迟执行 (LIFO)\nfunc readFile() {\n    f, err := os.Open(\"config.json\")\n    if err != nil {\n        fmt.Println(\"Error:\", err)\n        return\n    }\n    defer f.Close() // 函数返回前关闭文件\n\n    // ... 使用 f\n    fmt.Println(\"File processed\")\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    result, err := divide(10, 3)\n    if err != nil {\n        fmt.Println(\"Error:\", err)\n    } else {\n        fmt.Printf(\"10/3 = %.4f\\n\", result)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.2 闭包与匿名函数\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\n// 闭包\nfunc intSequence() func() int {\n    i := 0\n    return func() int {\n        i++\n        return i\n    }\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    // 匿名函数\n    add := func(a, b int) int {\n        return a + b\n    }\n    fmt.Println(add(3, 4)) // 7\n\n    // 闭包\n    nextInt := intSequence()\n    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 1\n    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 2\n    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 3\n}\n```\n\n## 四、结构体与方法\n\n### 4.1 结构体定义\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\ntype User struct {\n    Name  string\n    Email string\n    Age   int\n    Active bool\n}\n\n// 方法 (接收者)\nfunc (u *User) Greet() string {\n    return fmt.Sprintf(\"Hi, I'm %s!\", u.Name)\n}\n\nfunc (u User) Info() string {\n    return fmt.Sprintf(\"%s <u.%s> is %d\", u.Name, u.Email, u.Age)\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    user := User{\n        Name:   \"Alice\",\n        Email:  \"alice@example.com\",\n        Age:    30,\n        Active: true,\n    }\n\n    // 方法调用\n    fmt.Println(user.Greet())\n    fmt.Println(user.Info())\n\n    // 指针接收者允许修改\n    user.Name = \"Bob\"\n    fmt.Println(user.Greet())\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 嵌入与继承 (组合)\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\ntype Engine struct {\n    HP int\n}\n\nfunc (e Engine) Start() {\n    fmt.Printf(\"Engine started, %d HP\\n\", e.HP)\n}\n\ntype Car struct {\n    Brand string\n    Engine // 嵌入 - 提升方法\n}\n\nfunc (c Car) Drive() {\n    c.Start() // 通过嵌入直接调用\n    fmt.Printf(\"%s is driving\\n\", c.Brand)\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    car := Car{\n        Brand:  \"Tesla\",\n        Engine: Engine{HP: 500},\n    }\n    car.Drive()\n}\n```\n\n## 五、接口\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\n// 接口定义\ntype Stringer interface {\n    String() string\n}\n\ntype IntSlice []int\n\nfunc (s IntSlice) String() string {\n    result := \"[\"\n    for i, v := range s {\n        if i > 0 {\n            result += \", \"\n        }\n        result += fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", v)\n    }\n    return result + \"]\"\n}\n\n// 鸭子类型：实现即满足\ntype Shape interface {\n    Area() float64\n    Perimeter() float64\n}\n\ntype Circle struct {\n    Radius float64\n}\n\nfunc (c Circle) Area() float64 {\n    return 3.14159 * c.Radius * c.Radius\n}\n\nfunc (c Circle) Perimeter() float64 {\n    return 2 * 3.14159 * c.Radius\n}\n\ntype Rect struct {\n    W, H float64\n}\n\nfunc (r Rect) Area() float64      { return r.W * r.H }\nfunc (r Rect) Perimeter() float64 { return 2 * (r.W + r.H) }\n\nfunc describe(s Shape) {\n    fmt.Printf(\"Area: %.2f, Perimeter: %.2f\\n\", s.Area(), s.Perimeter())\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    numbers := IntSlice{1, 2, 3, 4, 5}\n    fmt.Println(numbers) // [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\n\n    describe(Circle{Radius: 5})\n    describe(Rect{W: 3, H: 4})\n}\n```\n\n## 六、错误处理\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"fmt\"\n    \"errors\"\n    \"net/url\"\n)\n\nvar ErrUserNotFound = errors.New(\"user not found\")\n\n// 自定义错误类型\ntype ValidationError struct {\n    Field  string\n    Reason string\n}\n\nfunc (e *ValidationError) Error() string {\n    return fmt.Sprintf(\"validation failed on %s: %s\", e.Field, e.Reason)\n}\n\nfunc validateEmail(email string) error {\n    if email == \"\" {\n        return &ValidationError{Field: \"email\", Reason: \"required\"}\n    }\n    if len(email) < 3 {\n        return &ValidationError{Field: \"email\", Reason: \"too short\"}\n    }\n    return nil\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    // 标准模式: 检查 error\n    result, err := validateEmail(\"a@b\")\n    if err != nil {\n        var vErr *ValidationError\n        if errors.As(err, &vErr) {\n            fmt.Printf(\"Error in field '%s': %s\\n\", vErr.Field, vErr.Reason)\n        }\n        return\n    }\n    fmt.Println(\"Valid:\", result)\n\n    // URL 解析\n    parsed, err := url.Parse(\"https://example.com/path?q=1\")\n    if err != nil {\n        fmt.Println(\"Parse error:\", err)\n    } else {\n        fmt.Println(\"Scheme:\", parsed.Scheme)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## 七、并发：goroutine 与 channel\n\n### 7.1 goroutine 基础\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"fmt\"\n    \"sync\"\n)\n\nfunc worker(id int, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {\n    defer wg.Done()\n    for i := 0; i < 3; i++ {\n        fmt.Printf(\"Worker %d: step %d\\n\", id, i)\n    }\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    var wg sync.WaitGroup\n\n    for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {\n        wg.Add(1)\n        go worker(i, &wg) // 启动 goroutine\n    }\n\n    wg.Wait() // 等待所有 goroutine 完成\n    fmt.Println(\"All workers done\")\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 Channel 通信\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"fmt\"\n)\n\n// 管道生产-消费\nfunc producer(ch chan<- int) {\n    for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {\n        ch <- i * i\n    }\n    close(ch)\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    ch := make(chan int, 3) // 带缓冲的 channel\n\n    // 启动生产者\n    go producer(ch)\n\n    // 消费\n    for val := range ch {\n        fmt.Println(\"Received:\", val)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 7.3 select 多路复用\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"fmt\"\n    \"time\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n    ch1 := make(chan string)\n    ch2 := make(chan string)\n\n    go func() {\n        time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)\n        ch1 <- \"from ch1\"\n    }()\n\n    go func() {\n        time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)\n        ch2 <- \"from ch2\"\n    }()\n\n    for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {\n        select {\n        case msg := <-ch1:\n            fmt.Println(\"Got:\", msg)\n        case msg := <-ch2:\n            fmt.Println(\"Got:\", msg)\n        case <-time.After(300 * time.Millisecond):\n            fmt.Println(\"Timeout!\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## 八、包与模块\n\n```go\n// go.mod\nmodule example.com/myapp\n\ngo 1.21\n\nrequire (\n    github.com/gorilla/mux v1.8.0\n)\n```\n\n```go\n// 导入标准库\npackage main\n\nimport (\n    \"encoding/json\"\n    \"fmt\"\n    \"net/http\"\n)\n\ntype Config struct {\n    Port    int    `json:\"port\"`\n    Debug   bool   `json:\"debug\"`\n    Workers []string `json:\"workers\"`\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    // JSON 解码\n    data := []byte(`{\"port\":8080,\"debug\":true,\"workers\":[\"w1\",\"w2\"]}`)\n    var cfg Config\n    if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cfg); err != nil {\n        fmt.Println(\"Error:\", err)\n        return\n    }\n\n    // HTTP 服务器\n    http.HandleFunc(\"/config\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n        w.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n        json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(cfg)\n    })\n\n    fmt.Println(\"Server starting on port\", cfg.Port)\n    http.ListenAndServe(fmt.Sprintf(\":%d\", cfg.Port), nil)\n}\n```\n\n## 九、泛型 (Go 1.18+)\n\n```go\npackage main\n\nimport \"fmt\"\n\n// 泛型函数\nfunc Map[T any, U any](slice []T, f func(T) U) []U {\n    result := make([]U, len(slice))\n    for i, v := range slice {\n        result[i] = f(v)\n    }\n    return result\n}\n\n// 约束接口\ntype Number interface {\n    int | int32 | int64 | float32 | float64\n}\n\nfunc Sum[T Number](nums []T) T {\n    var total T\n    for _, n := range nums {\n        total += n\n    }\n    return total\n}\n\nfunc main() {\n    // 字符串处理\n    words := []string{\"hello\", \"world\", \"go\"}\n    upper := Map(words, func(s string) string {\n        return s + \"!\"\n    })\n    fmt.Println(upper) // [hello! world! go!]\n\n    // 数值求和\n    ints := []int{1, 2, 3, 4, 5}\n    fmt.Println(Sum(ints)) // 15\n\n    floats := []float64{1.1, 2.2, 3.3}\n    fmt.Println(Sum(floats)) // 6.6\n}\n```\n\n## 十、推荐学习资源\n\n| 资源 | 类型 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|\n| Go 官方 Learn 页 | 教程 | go.dev/learn - 官方入门 |\n| A Tour of Go | 交互 | go.dev/tour - 在线练习 |\n| Go 用户手册 | 参考 | go.dev/doc |\n| 100天精通Golang | 系列 | 阿里云开发者社区 |\n| Go Wiki Training | 导航 | go.dev/wiki/Training |\n| The Go Programming Language | 书籍 | 官方推荐 |\n\n## 十一、最佳实践\n\n1. **错误即值**：`if err != nil` 是 Go 的惯用法，不吞错误\n2. **小函数**：函数职责单一，不超过 50 行\n3. **避免循环依赖**：包之间单向依赖\n4. **接口小而精**：消费者定义接口，1-2 个方法为宜\n5. **并发用 channel 通信**：避免共享内存，减少锁使用\n6. **遵循 Go fmt 格式化**：`gofmt` 统一代码风格\n7. **文档注释**：导出标识符必须有 `//` 注释（godoc 格式）\n", "path": "E:\\test_dir\\go_manual.md"}`

示例代码\n-\n-```go\n-package main\n-\n-import "fmt"\n-\n-// 结构体\n-type Animal struct {\n+    defer f.Close() // 函数返回前关闭文件\n+\n+    // ... 使用 f\n+    fmt.Println("File processed")\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    result, err := divide(10, 3)\n+    if err != nil {\n+        fmt.Println("Error:", err)\n+    } else {\n+        fmt.Printf("10/3 = %.4f\\n", result)\n+    }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 3.2 闭包与匿名函数\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import "fmt"\n+\n+// 闭包\n+func intSequence() func() int {\n+    i := 0\n+    return func() int {\n+        i++\n+        return i\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    // 匿名函数\n+    add := func(a, b int) int {\n+        return a + b\n+    }\n+    fmt.Println(add(3, 4)) // 7\n+\n+    // 闭包\n+    nextInt := intSequence()\n+    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 1\n+    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 2\n+    fmt.Println(nextInt()) // 3\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 四、结构体与方法\n+\n+### 4.1 结构体定义\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import "fmt"\n+\n+type User struct {\n     Name  string\n-    Legs  int\n-    Sound string\n-}\n-\n-// 值接收者方法\n-func (a Animal) Describe() string {\n-    return fmt.Sprintf("%s with %d legs says %s", a.Name, a.Legs, a.Sound)\n-}\n-\n-// 指针接收者方法(可修改)\n-func (a fmt.Println(pet.Describe())\n-}\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 5. 接口\n-\n-### 核心概念\n-Go接口是隐式实现的（鸭子类型），类型只要实现了接口方法就自动满足接口。空接口 `interface{}`/`any` 可接受任何类型。\n-\n-### 示例代码\n-\n-```go\n-package main\n-\n-import "fmt"\n-\n-// 定义接口\n-type Reader interface {\n-    Read() string\n-}\n-\n-type Writer interface {\n-    Write(msg string)\n-}\n-\n-type ReadWriter interface {\n-    Reader\n-    Writer\n-}\n-\n-// 实现结构体\n-type File struct {\n-    Content string\n-}\n-\n-func (f *File) Read() string {\n-    return f.Content\n-}\n-\n-func (f *File) Write(msg string) {\n-    f.Content = msg\n-}\n-\n-type Console struct{}\n-\n-func (c *Console) Read() string     { return "console input" }\n-func (c *Console) Write(msg string)  { fmt.Println(msg) }\n-\n-// 使用接口(多态)\n-func printInfo(r Reader) {\n-    fmt.Println("Read:", r.Read())\n-}\n-\n-func main() {\n-    f := &File{Content: "Hello from file"}\n-    c := &Console{}\n-\n-    printInfo(f)  // 传入任何 Reader 实现\n-    printInfo(c)\n-\n-    // 类型断言\n-    var rw ReadWriter = f\n-    if reader, ok := rw.(Reader); ok {\n-        fmt.Println("Has Read:", reader.Read())\n-    }\n-}\n-```\n-\n----\n-\n-## 6. 错误处理\n-\n-### 核心概念\n-Go不使用异常，而是通过返回 `error` 类型处理错误。`errors.New` 和 `fmt.Errorf` 创建错误。\n-\n-### 示例代码\n+    Email string\n+    Age   int\n+    Active bool\n+}\n+\n+// 方法 (接收者)\n+func (u *User) Greet() string {\n+    return fmt.Sprintf("Hi, I\'m %s!", u.Name)\n+}\n+\n+func (u User) Info() string {\n+    return fmt.Sprintf("%s <u.%s> is %d", u.Name, u.Email, u.Age)\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    user := User{\n+        Name:   "Alice",\n+        Email:  "alice@example.com",\n+        Age:    30,\n+        Active: true,\n+    }\n+\n+    // 方法调用\n+    fmt.Println(user.Greet())\n+    fmt.Println(user.Info())\n+\n+    // 指针接收者允许修改\n+    user.Name = "Bob"\n+    fmt.Println(user.Greet())\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 4.2 嵌入与继承 (组合)\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import "fmt"\n+\n+type Engine struct {\n+    HP int\n+}\n+\n+func (e Engine) Start() {\n+    fmt.Printf("Engine started, %d HP\\n", e.HP)\n+}\n+\n+type Car struct {\n+    Brand string\n+    Engine // 嵌入 - 提升方法\n+}\n+\n+func (c Car) Drive() {\n+    c.Start() // 通过嵌入直接调用\n+    fmt.Printf("%s is driving\\n", c.Brand)\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    car := Car{\n+        Brand:  "Tesla",\n+        Engine: Engine{HP: 500},\n+    }\n+    car.Drive()\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 五、接口\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import "fmt"\n+\n+// 接口定义\n+type Stringer interface {\n+    String() string\n+}\n+\n+type IntSlice []int\n+\n+func (s IntSlice) String() string {\n+    result := "["\n+    for i, v := range s {\n+        if i > 0 {\n+            result += ", "\n+        }\n+        result += fmt.Sprintf("%d", v)\n+    }\n+    return result + "]"\n+}\n+\n+// 鸭子类型：实现即满足\n+type Shape interface {\n+    Area() float64\n+    Perimeter() float64\n+}\n+\n+type Circle struct {\n+    Radius float64\n+}\n+\n+func (c Circle) Area() float64 {\n+    return 3.14159 * c.Radius * c.Radius\n+}\n+\n+func (c Circle) Perimeter() float64 {\n+    return 2 * 3.14159 * c.Radius\n+}\n+\n+type Rect struct {\n+    W, H float64\n+}\n+\n+func (r Rect) Area() float64      { return r.W * r.H }\n+func (r Rect) Perimeter() float64 { return 2 * (r.W + r.H) }\n+\n+func describe(s Shape) {\n+    fmt.Printf("Area: %.2f, Perimeter: %.2f\\n", s.Area(), s.Perimeter())\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    numbers := IntSlice{1, 2, 3, 4, 5}\n+    fmt.Println(numbers) // [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\n+\n+    describe(Circle{Radius: 5})\n+    describe(Rect{W: 3, H: 4})\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 六、错误处理\n \n ```go\n package main\n \n import (\n+    "fmt"\n     "errors"\n-    "fmt"\n+    "net/url"\n )\n \n-// 预定义错误\n-var (\n-    ErrNotFound      = errors.New("item not found")\n-    ErrPermission    = errors.New("permission denied")\n-)\n-\n-// 带上下文的错误 (Go 1.13+ %w)\n-type Config struct{}\n-\n-func (c *Config) Load() error {\n-    err := c.readFile()\n+var ErrUserNotFound = errors.New("user not found")\n+\n+// e.Reason)\n+}\n+\n+func validateEmail(email string) error {\n+    if email == "" {\n+        return &ValidationError{Field: "email", Reason: "required"}\n+    }\n+    if len(email) < 3 {\n+        return &ValidationError{Field: "email", Reason: "too short"}\n+    }\n+    return nil\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    // 标准模式: 检查 error\n+    result, err := validateEmail("a@b")\n     if err != nil {\n- Goroutine 与 Channel\n-\n-### 核心概念\n-Goroutine是轻量级并发单元（协程），Channel是类型安全的通信管道。CSP模型（Communicating Sequential Processes）。\n-\n-### 示例代码\n+        var vErr *ValidationError\n+        if errors.As(err, &vErr) {\n+            fmt.Printf("Error in field \'%s\': %s\\n", vErr.Field, vErr.Reason)\n+        }\n+        return\n+    }\n+    fmt.Println("Valid:", result)\n+\n+    // URL 解析\n+    parsed, err := url.Parse("https://example.com/path?q=1")\n+    if err != nil {\n+        fmt.Println("Parse error:", err)\n+    } else {\n+        fmt.Println("Scheme:", parsed.Scheme)\n+    }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 七、并发：goroutine 与 channel\n+\n+### 7.1 goroutine 基础\n \n ```go\n package main\n@@ -389,122 +427,207 @@\n import (\n     "fmt"\n     "sync"\n+)\n+\n+func worker(id int, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {\n+    defer wg.Done()\n+    for i := 0; i < 3; i++ {\n+        fmt.Printf("Worker %d: step %d\\n", id, i)\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    var wg sync.WaitGroup\n+\n+    for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {\n+        wg.Add(1)\n+        go worker(i, &wg) // 启动 goroutine\n+    }\n+\n+    wg.Wait() // 等待所有 goroutine 完成\n+    fmt.Println("All workers done")\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 7.2 Channel 通信\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import (\n+    "fmt"\n+)\n+\n+// 管道生产-消费\n+func producer(ch chan<- int) {\n+    for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {\n+        ch <- i * i\n+    }\n+    close(ch)\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    ch := make(chan int, 3) // 带缓冲的 channel\n+\n+    // 启动生产者\n+    go producer(ch)\n+\n+    // 消费\n+    for val := range ch {\n+        fmt.Println("Received:", val)\n+    }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+### 7.3 select 多路复用\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import (\n+    "fmt"\n     "time"\n )\n \n-// 基本goroutine\n-func sayHello(msg string, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {\n-    defer wg.Done()\n-    time.Sleep(100 * 示例代码\n-\n-```go\n+    go func() {\n+        time.Sleep(50 * time.Millisecond)\n+        ch2 <- "from ch2"\n+    }()\n+\n+    for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {\n+        select {\n+        case msg := <-ch1:\n+            fmt.Println("Got:", msg)\n+        case msg := <-ch2:\n+            fmt.Println("Got:", msg)\n+        case <-time.After(300 * time.Millisecond):\n+            fmt.Println("Timeout!")\n+        }\n+    }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 八、包与模块\n+\n+```go\n+// go.mod\n+module example.com/myapp\n+\n+go 1.21\n+\n+require (\n+    github.com/gorilla/mux v1.8.0\n+)\n+```\n+\n+```go\n+// 导入标准库\n package `json:"workers"`\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    // JSON 解码\n+    data := []byte(`{"port":8080,"debug":true,"workers":["w1","w2"]}`)\n+    var cfg Config\n+    if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cfg); err != nil {\n+        fmt.Println("Error:", err)\n+        return\n+    }\n+\n+    // HTTP 服务器\n+    http.HandleFunc("/config", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n+        w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")\n+        json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(cfg)\n+    })\n+\n+    fmt.Println("Server starting on port", cfg.Port)\n+    http.ListenAndServe(fmt.Sprintf(":%d", cfg.Port), nil)\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 九、泛型 (Go 1.18+)\n+\n+```go\n+package main\n+\n+import "fmt"\n+\n+// 泛型函数\n+func Map[T any, U any](slice []T, f func(T) U) []U {\n+    result := make([]U, len(slice))\n+    for i, v := range slice {\n+        result[i] = f(v)\n+    }\n+    return result\n+}\n+\n+// 约束接口\n+type Number interface {\n+    int | int32 | int64 | float32 | float64\n+}\n+\n+func Sum[T Number](nums []T) T {\n+    var total T\n+    for _, n := range nums {\n+        total += n\n+    }\n+    return total\n+}\n+\n+func main() {\n+    // 字符串处理\n+    words := []string{"hello", "world", "go"}\n+    upper := Map(words, func(s string) string {\n+        return s + "!"\n+    })\n+    fmt.Println(upper) // [hello! world! go!]\n+\n+    // 数值求和\n+    ints := []int{1, 2, 3, 4, 5}\n+    fmt.Println(Sum(ints)) // 15\n+\n+    floats := []float64{1.1, 2.2, 3.3}\n+    fmt.Println(Sum(floats)) // 6.6\n+}\n+```\n+\n+## 十、推荐学习资源\n+\n+| 资源 | 类型 | 说明 **避免循环依赖**：包之间单向依赖\n+4. **接口小而精**：消费者定义接口，1-2 个方法为宜\n+5. **并发用 channel 通信**：避免共享内存，减少锁使用\n+6. **遵循 Go fmt 格式化**：`gofmt` 统一代码风格\n+7. **文档注释**：导出标识符必须有 `//` 注释（godoc 格式）\n'}, 'data_text': '工具执行: 写文件
